[23/06/2025 10:14 pm] 0_____________________________.0
Rather than waiting for nothing, I advise you to step out, look at the situation properly, and put yourself on another hypothetical. The train is not the good way out. I've seen many train accidents live and they're not pretty and the victim dies a very painful death. You won't just get splattered into a pancake, you won't just be split in half or you might not even have the time for your head to get under the tracks. And the worst part of it is you'll be conscious for all of it up until you die with blood in your throat from either the impact of the train, or the split torso; your final breath with will be filled with blood and a pulsating headache. 

2 weeks is not a very small but not a very large time frame either. I advise you to acknowledge your own feelings and try to explain why your body is feeling these feelings you currently are feeling. 
Why? You're feeling this immense pressure, this weight. This isn't a simple decision. This is your brain running a recursive feedback loop. You feel a pang of depression—a hormonal dip—and your mind, seeking a reason, latches onto the grandest, most dramatic explanation: "I must want to die.| This thought then validates the feeling, which in turn deepens the thought. You're stuck in a logical loop created by a physical feeling. The challenge isn't to act on the conclusion, but to break the loop itself. Your body is creating a feeling, and your mind is telling a story to match it. What if the story is wrong?


[23/06/2025 10:16 pm] 0_____________________________.0
Give yourself some more time to process and think about the journey this feeling will lead you to. Then depending on how this will go, you'll have your answer whether or not you will take things in your own hands or not.

[23/06/2025 11:17 pm] 0_____________________________.0
So things happened, and you're actively trying to process your breakup with them and are still friends with them. 

From what I can tell the breakup was against your ideation of your future and it's slowly falling apart because you hadn't imagined a world without them. The breakup was also the realisation on the both of you that you don't specifically match the life values and have different opinions on the outlook on life. 

You are still friends with them and are hoping to once again reconcile and be in a relationship again => this can only happen if: 
1. one of you made a big mistake
2. one of you is immature (in a sense that the relationship will not be long term) to escalate it again
3. They are an asshole and wants to use your feelings for their own amusement. 

Realistically, reaping ripe fruits from rotten crops is unachievable. You will need to move on in any way or another. You'll eventually grow distant despite being friends and (possibly) will contact each in the next stage of life or never. 

I'm telling you to (cry and) move on because you both (or one of you) realized this relationship was going nowhere. You deserve another chance at the dating pool, with ones that share your values and accept you for who you are, supporting you at your weakest; rather than already planning a big wedding with expensive rings or a mansion with 10 children or some type of shit. 

You are still emotionally unwell, and I have a feeling that you're still harbouring some of the leftover feelings you have left for them. As I've said before 2 days ago, letting it go is important but giving up is something be learned, not mandated.

So this is somthing i wrote rate it:


You keep me second-guessing,
Wasting in an uncertain void,
Floating and regretting,
Listening to the deafening noise.

In all my years, I wandered —
Do I belong?
It is why I am unencumbered.
It’s why I am writing this wretched song.

No one heard my screams —
The screams, they echoed in the night.
All they saw was a remnant of a dream,
A dream about my last fight.

I can’t go on like this.
I can’t do this anymore.
I want to not miss —
Miss the things I had before.

This isn’t changing anytime soon.
On my chest, my heart — there’s a wound.
It cannot heal, but fuck it hurts.

I wanted to be noticed and seen.
I wanted to be respected and loved.
All I got was hits of dopamine,
But I never was beloved.

Now I’m waiting — that things will change.
Now I’m waiting, hopeless and in pain.
This has to end, this deranged —
’Cause all the waiting was in vain.

Ahh, I think it’s time to say goodbye now.
The train is coming down the track.
This is the last time I sigh.
Goodbye now — don’t wait. I won’t be back.

2. verse: reasons of void. 
3. verse: void effects. 
4. verse: void defects. 
5. verse: Acknowledging the persistence of the void. 
6. verse: A cry for help despite the void. 
7. verse: Patience of the void. 
8. verse: the eventuality and the pitfall, and most likely the downfall of the person crying for help.

It has the desperation of someone trying to get out from the blanket trap the "person" under it is in. 

Overall for me 6.5/10, not my type, realistically more or less 7.2/10.

[05/07/2025 4:15 pm] 0_____________________________.0
Like you know people with depression struggle with everyday tasks like memorizing because they can't associate a fact with an emotion so you can't remember when you're depressed.


[05/07/2025 4:16 pm] 0_____________________________.0
So let's say depression is the opposite of investment and emotional attachment, so in that case you'll get full benefits of remembering everything from bad to good.


[05/07/2025 4:17 pm] 0_____________________________.0
Oh and the judgement of a fact if its good or bad also contributes to your proficiency in memory


[05/07/2025 4:19 pm] 0_____________________________.0
So the main question becomes, how do I become emotionally attached and invested in something I don't really understand or like?


[05/07/2025 4:20 pm] 0_____________________________.0
And a neat little trick is, treat objects like people and people like objects.


[05/07/2025 4:20 pm] 0_____________________________.0
But if you're emotionally attached to everything, how would you expect to remember everything?


[05/07/2025 4:20 pm] 0_____________________________.0
And the answer to that question is, contrast. Focus on what you want to invest your energy on and spend emotional points on.

[11/07/2025 4:34 am] zipherious
even when I wake up tired
when I don't know what I desired
this time I won't care

maybe it's all too sudden
maybe I'm a glutton
but the stone was too much to bare

finally I see the light
shining down, ending the night
I can say that I moved on
so the darkness can be gone

the struggle that haunted
and the scar that it produced
I got the relief that I wanted
and the pain was reduced

back then I could only see darkness
engulfing everything in black
my mind was in internal blankness
and I don't want to go back

now I turn a new page
start a brand new age
living with an open gate
striving to carry on my fate

[15/07/2025 4:56 pm] 0_____________________________.0
well yeah, it's a matter of expectation vs reality. the more public your awareness is, the more performative you get. You don't want other's to get the wrong idea about you right? people expect you to be normal, so you perform. 
once you get familiar with someone, the phase of familiarity; it's more comfortable to be real with the person you're with because you know the person who is with you will accept you and you will accept them for being real. THIS is where the battle of your Expectation with the reality collides. You're no longer the author of your own expectations of that single person - you're now adapting to that person and he is adapting to you.

which brings me to a point and a question. 
People who say self-awareness is a blessing and a curse are aware enough to observe their own thoughts about self awareness, but lack self awareness based on why it's a curse or a blessing. The question is, if the self-awareness is:
- a tool; a measure that dictates and helps you decide on what your next course action is, or 
- a diagnosis; a report as a result of introspection and self reflection, granting awareness, but not the tool you have to make decisions with.

[22/07/2025 6:42 pm] 0_____________________________.0
I just realized Autism is opposite of being wise. 
Let me explain. 
So it's known that autists have been known to have trouble with social awareness and communication and basically anything that isn't up in their interest. 
Let's suppose: 
1. You can be genetically wise. This means you don't lack the directional focus for you to get a grasp of your own life and can focus on individual things without undermining your basic needs at a subconscious level.
2. You can learn wisdom. This is separate from genetic wisdom and requires a lot of guidance, commitment from parents for you to be able to function on your own at all times. Sure you might have needed time to adjust to what you want and what you need, but ultimately it's similar to being genetically wise. 
3. Autism is:
 3.1. A genetic neurological mutation, quite frankly has been a parallel to our societal lives. Where since at birth the child was unable to focus on what they're essentially forced to focus on, at the survival level. This focus, or rather lack thereof, because a certain topic doesn't motivate them to think about it, pairs them well with diagnosing them with ADHD, which is not entirely false (but I reckon that nobody has ADHD in a sense, it's just that the attention they need for a certain priority is misplaced) but puts them categorically worse compared to their peers. 
3.2. A lack of interest towards multiple subjects, or more concretely a lazer focus towards their own unshakable topic. 
3.3. It's just parents fault not being able to teach their kid what to think about, how to sort priorities and whatnot.


[22/07/2025 6:42 pm] 0_____________________________.0
With that said, it is a spectrum. Everybody is born different, and everybody was raised different. 
I can argue, that high functioning autists are statistically more prone to high functioning autism because they lack inner guidance or that parental figure didn't etch out their path towards their self-completeness. 
I can argue, Autism is a brain's way to take a shortcut to whatever subject it wants to focus on. 
I can argue, that low functioning autists are statistically more likely to be a result of genetical mutations in their neurological pathways, or arguably lack thereof to judge their own path without undermining their basic needs or ever higher needs. 
I can argue, Wisdom is an antonym of Autism in this regard. But same with Autism, wisdom is also a spectrum and I can't expect everyone to be wise in the same sense anyone can be autistic. 

I can conclude that Autism is a misplacement of attention, and wisdom is the art of organisation of such.

[23/07/2025 4:38 pm] 0_____________________________.0
that's the entire point of maturity - to treat other opinions as theirs and correcting when the subjective crosses the objective realm. 
There are, theoretically 2 interpretations 1 person can have, but since a person practically is made from subjective experience. Meanwhile you can practice having multiple opinions on a matter no matter how subjective your particular experience gets. And that's where you can access to control your own emotional wellbeing and what you can do about it.

**"thought of often"** is poetic, you should have it as a poetic phrase. 
So:
I would like to see
I would like to feel 
But with the ~~thought of me~~ thought of often
I dont wanna deal

I don't wanna deal is informal
Make it >I don't want a/this deal. 

All of it was a pharse 
Everything a lie
>> All of it is a phrase, 
>> a lie. (all of it = everything)
>> Fake laughter, 
>> sense of happiness, (it adds more impact if you sensed it was happiness rather than the feeling of it being fake)...

The smile i once had 
The darkness ripped it out
>> I once had a smile, now masked by darkness,
>> It questioned, it screamed,
>> Why such smile, 
>> if I'm a mess?  (or some shit)

Next verse:

This isnt ending but
for it to be over,
I want it to be over,
to end.
be happy.
be loved,
I want...
I want to be seen
Feels just like a dream
Nothing has ever mattered

Falling deeper unto the void, 
silence is deafening, 
but the smallest noise
never falls to its end. 
It will always persist,
last stand here will never be.




Notes:
- try to omit repetition (althought repitition can be poetic), adds flavour and more interpetational freedom.
- write in the present tense, unless you're building a story. 
- use punctuations to make your poem rhythmical. 
- try to use more encapsulating words that replace entire sentences. (Like "I feel very empty inside" -> Melancholy dissolved me or some shit)
-

[05/08/2025 9:39 pm] 0_____________________________.0
You keep me second-guessing,
Wasting in an uncertain void,
Floating and regretting,
Listening to the deafening noise.

In all my years, I wandered —
Do I belong?
It is why I am unencumbered.
It’s why I am writing this wretched song.

No one heard my screams —
The screams, they echoed in the night.
All they saw was a remnant of a dream,
A dream about my last fight.

I can’t go on like this.
I can’t do this anymore.
I want to not miss —
Miss the things I had before.

This isn’t changing anytime soon.
On my chest, my heart — there’s a wound.
It cannot heal, but fuck it hurts.

I wanted to be noticed and seen.
I wanted to be respected and loved.
All I got was hits of dopamine,
But I never was beloved.

Now I’m waiting — that things will change.
Now I’m waiting, hopeless and in pain.
This has to end, this deranged —
’Cause all the waiting was in vain.

Ahh, I think it’s time to say goodbye now.
The train is coming down the track.
This is the last time I sigh.
Goodbye now — don’t wait. I won’t be back.

[10/08/2025 11:37 am] 0_____________________________.0
Your task is to 
1. Match the emotional experience with another equally or more emotional stimuli **directly** in *response* to that emotional stimulus. 
2. Assess that your emotional constraints are valid and are perfectly normal for any person. 
3. Acknowledge, that this will always be a part of your life's history, whether you like it or not. 
4. Accept that you're going to be hurt again so you can prepare yourself for that. 

What I mean by matching emotional stimulus is: 
1. Cry, like a lot. (althought me telling you to be aware of your crying, strips you off actual emotional attachment trying to detach yourself from that stimulus)
2. Let it motivate and fuel you to do better in the future, like poems, write, or something similar. 
3. Punch things, 
4. Vent, 
5. Rant, 
6. Run, jog as far as you can, 
7. Drink a lot of water. 
8. Study what went wrong, justify your actions. 


Note that little what you did and the result of what you got is your fault. You lost control for a while, so regain it back at your own pace using the matching stimulus actions. 

Do not deprecate yourself further by shifting blame on to yourself. If you can't forgive, blame anyone involved around you, then you can start justifying your own, then theirs.

[10/08/2025 7:29 pm] 0_____________________________.0
You think whatever your dealing with, the impact it had on you let's set that to "-L" (for loss) class or variable. 
And other impactful things, let's call them "C" (counterloss/cope), like crying, venting, or other coping mechanisms. 
I'll also add "S" for suicide, as an additional context. 

I'll reframe the context:

1. You're saying that your Cs aren't equal or bigger than the L you experienced, but the only way you're feeling you could solve this is with is an S, which stands bigger and/or equal to C and L. 

 - So you'd be saying S>L>C and that S could solve your problems because S towers over L and C. 

2. positive impacts are prefixed with + and negative with -, in this case -L would be considered negative, +L would have given you the opposite of -L, which is the antonym of loss, in your case. 

3. Let's assume the compromise O is a range from 0 or larger, which would assume you have a neutral or positive outcome. 

 - In your position, 
 -> -L > C 
 -> -S > -L  (because S is considered always to be negative)
 therefore -> -L>C 

Here's the equation: 
you're trying to match the coping C (valued C) with the same energy as the problem L (valued -L) so in order to equalize, in an ideal world, yield a net 0 or positive outcome.  C - (-L) >=0  
> but this isn't the case, because the C isn't as big as -(-L). 

You spiral with your thoughts onto the lateral space and think of a solution that could solve your problems, S (valued -S) 
SO: (-S) - (-L) >= 0 
but here's the problem. 

there isn't a positive S or you'd literally have to invent resurrection for this to be true at certain times, this equation will never hold true either. 
> No matter how small your -S would have been, the outcome would have never been positive, neither for you, nor for anybody around you. 

Proposal: 
You find a meaningful C, that will successfully counteract the L. 
If the previous statement doesn't hold true, find another variable to find meaning in other places
else if, you live with it


[10/08/2025 7:30 pm] 0_____________________________.0
And by finding another variable, I mean something new that will give you a positive outcome at another topic, another genre, another book in your life's chapter.


[10/08/2025 7:31 pm] 0_____________________________.0
and by living with it, is by living with the negative effects you are having right now, learning from it would make it so that eventually you'll turn this equation positive.

[14/08/2025 11:16 am] 0_____________________________.0
It's not that I'm against you having a relationship, I'm just against you having a partner you don't know very well to the point that you could have trusted with your life and are prepared to give your life for them, if the situation is fit for that. 

I'm telling you to channel the relationship goal somewhere else, to the people who don't seem so "disposable", who can't and won't (legally or physically) walk out of your life. I'm telling you to have a relationship with yourself, set goals and see yourself in a light only you can imagine seeing yourself in. I'm telling you to have a relationship with your parents, the one's who (I imagine) you trust and could trust with your trust. I'm telling you to have a relationship with your siblings, closer relatives, and so on, so that the purpose of constant branching out doesn't wear you out, when meeting new people. 

You're meeting new people, they can and will walk away if they just think about it, unless you give them a good reason not to walk out. Gay relationships are especially more volatile because of the ambiguity of acceptance, the uncertainty of gay marriages, and the overall vibe around having a gay partner in your family, both for you and for your partner. 

The goal of you having a goal or meaning IN a relationship is nice, but the whole idea is building upon the laid foundations, not creating the foundations. You're committing to a relationship because you see something has been built on the foundations not because they're a blank slate. The foundations can be later altered, guided and reconstructed. Not created. The purpose of foundation creation goes to your parents and parents alone, that's why you were raised.  You don't need another mom.

Relationships are for common goals to be realized. If you have no sense of direction, if both of you seem to be lost, If one of you eventually finds purpose in life, things will feel lonely again.

ruminating around the rocks.

starving and forgiving

in the tidal dense.

nostalgic film of regrets.

reset and free,

[31/08/2025 3:12 pm] 0_____________________________.0
I'd like to propose a new word: infermation; derivement from the word information, which is a word for a statement or a set of meaningful words that give and provide meaning or description of a certain, object; often set within the informational context. 
Infermation would be a derivement from information because it would look at the reason of meaning behind the 'being' rather than attributing value of meaning, we can attribute value of reason along with the contextual confidence score that usually might go along with infermation. 
Infermation solves a lot of ambiguity and accusatory language semanticism in terms of 'attacking' or arguing with another person, that would usually leave the other person vurnerable with the information he'd be accused of.
With infermation against you, you'd be inferred to be the "inferred", e.g. like labels of pleading homophobia when you're in a debate against gay relationship, when in fact there is no plausible evidence that would suggest that.
Infermation forces people to think about information and infermation itself whether an argument is being supported by informational reasoning (research with scientific evidence or reliable sources) or infermational leaps of judgement (jumping to conclusions) made by the inferrent.
